					<description>AWS Quick gives your whole organization AI that knows your business, respects your security rules, and acts on your behalf. At AWS re:Invent 2026, customers including Allianz, Cox Automotive, and Southwest Airlines share how they govern, extend, and scale Quick. Use this guide to find the sessions that fit your goals.</description>
										<content:encoded>&lt;p&gt;AWS Quick is the AI workspace built only for work, where the dashboards, agents, and automations one person creates are available to everyone on the team. Built on AWS with enterprise security and governance from the start, Quick connects to the data and applications you use every day. At AWS re:Invent 2026 (November 30 to December 4), customers including Allianz, Cox Automotive, Mondelez, DXC, and Southwest Airlines, join AWS leaders on stage to share how they govern, extend and scale Quick across their teams. Whether you’re a business or technology leader, a builder, or part of an IT, security, data, or analytics team, use this guide to find the sessions that fit your goals.&lt;/p&gt; 
&lt;p&gt;&lt;a href="https://registration.awsevents.com/flow/awsevents/reinvent2026/eventcatalog/page/eventcatalog?search=quick" target="_blank" rel="noopener"&gt;Explore the session catalog and learn more&lt;/a&gt;.&lt;/p&gt; 
&lt;h2 id="keynote"&gt;Keynote&lt;/h2&gt; 
&lt;p&gt;Join AWS CEO Matt Garman for the re:Invent 2026 CEO Keynote on Tuesday from 8:30 AM – 10:30 AM PT as he shares the latest innovations powering the agentic era. From AI infrastructure and inference to data, agent platforms, and the software agents AWS is building, see how AWS gives you the depth, breadth, and integration to build what’s next with new announcements and stories from customers already doing it.&lt;/p&gt; 
&lt;p&gt;Can’t make it to Las Vegas? Watch 50+ sessions live on the &lt;a href="https://aws.amazon.com/events/reinvent/livestream/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"&gt;AWS re:Invent livestream&lt;/a&gt;, including keynotes, talks, and labs.&lt;/p&gt; 
&lt;h2 id="breakout-sessions"&gt;Breakout sessions&lt;/h2&gt; 
&lt;p&gt;Hear directly from customers about how they deployed AWS Quick, from first rollout to enterprise scale, and what they learned along the way.&lt;/p&gt; 
&lt;p&gt;&lt;strong&gt;&lt;a href="https://registration.awsevents.com/flow/awsevents/reinvent2026/eventcatalog/page/eventcatalog?search=BIZ216" target="_blank" rel="noopener"&gt;BIZ216: AI assistant for public sector: Governance without compromise with AWS Quick&lt;/a&gt;&lt;/strong&gt;&lt;br&gt; Monday, Nov 30 | 10:00 AM PST&lt;br&gt; &lt;em&gt;Speakers: Rima Olinger, Sonali Sahu, Mike Shore-Nye&lt;/em&gt;&lt;/p&gt; 
&lt;p&gt;Public sector organizations serve diverse populations with strict boundaries: students and administrators, citizens and case workers, patients and providers. They need AI that works across these groups without compromising data isolation, compliance, or governance. Multi-tenant environments demand security frameworks most AI assistants were not built to meet. Hear from a higher education customer who faced these constraints and displaced their incumbent tool in favor of AWS Quick’s governance-first architecture: tenant-level data isolation, role-based access controls across distinct user populations, and compliance frameworks built for regulated environments. Learn why existing solutions fell short, how Quick’s native governance model met their requirements without custom engineering, and what the transition looked like from decision to deployment.&lt;/p&gt; 
&lt;p&gt;&lt;strong&gt;&lt;a href="https://registration.awsevents.com/flow/awsevents/reinvent2026/eventcatalog/page/eventcatalog?search=BIZ219" target="_blank" rel="noopener"&gt;BIZ219: How a brand turned AI governance into a strategic advantage with AWS Quick&lt;/a&gt;&lt;/strong&gt;&lt;br&gt; Monday, Nov 30 | 10:00 AM PST&lt;br&gt; &lt;em&gt;Speakers:&lt;/em&gt; Steve Vinsik, Mark Huber, Katie Travis&lt;/p&gt; 
&lt;p&gt;In automotive, data isolation is not optional. You serve thousands of dealer clients who compete with each other, handle consumer financial data under FTC Safeguards rules, and operate across state privacy boundaries. Most AI tools cannot enforce tenant-level separation between competing dealers, restrict access by franchise agreement, or prove compliance across jurisdictions. Hear from a customer who turned that constraint into an advantage: they built a governance model with AWS Quick that enforces fine-grained data permissions across their dealer network, role-based access controls by business function, and audit frameworks that satisfy regulators and competitive separation requirements. Learn how they validated it against compliance requirements, and how governance became the reason they deployed faster than competitors still waiting on security approval.&lt;/p&gt; 
&lt;p&gt;&lt;strong&gt;&lt;a href="https://registration.awsevents.com/flow/awsevents/reinvent2026/eventcatalog/page/eventcatalog?search=BIZ112" target="_blank" rel="noopener"&gt;BIZ112: From zero to 20,000 desktops: Getting started with AWS Quick&lt;/a&gt;&lt;/strong&gt;&lt;br&gt; Monday, Nov 30 | 12:00 PM PST&lt;br&gt; &lt;em&gt;Speakers: Jose Kunnackal John&lt;/em&gt;&lt;/p&gt; 
&lt;p&gt;Your team is drowning in context-switching: toggling between email, documents, data tools, and chat only to complete a single task. Productivity tools promise to help, but most add complexity instead of removing it. In this fireside chat, hear from customers who faced that exact challenge and deployed an AI assistant across 20,000+ users in under 90 days. Learn what convinced their leadership to invest, how they integrated AI into daily workflows without disrupting existing tools, and what the first 90 days of adoption actually looked like. Walk away with a practical roadmap for bringing AWS Quick AI assistant to your workforce at scale.&lt;/p&gt; 
&lt;p&gt;&lt;strong&gt;&lt;a href="https://registration.awsevents.com/flow/awsevents/reinvent2026/eventcatalog/page/eventcatalog?search=BIZ214" target="_blank" rel="noopener"&gt;BIZ214: Empowering 200,000 people without compromising trust: AWS Quick&lt;/a&gt;&lt;/strong&gt;&lt;br&gt; Tuesday, Dec 1 | 4:30 PM PST&lt;br&gt; &lt;em&gt;Speakers: Ben Haller, Raji Sivasubramaniam, Axel Schell&lt;/em&gt;&lt;/p&gt; 
&lt;p&gt;Allianz needed to empower 200,000 employees with AI without sacrificing trust. With operations across dozens of regulated markets, the security, privacy, and works council requirements were non-negotiable. Every stakeholder asked the same question: how do you move fast and still earn the trust of those guarding your company’s security? Hear how Allianz partnered with AWS to deploy the capabilities that make AWS Quick enterprise-ready: protection of confidential information, structured approval workflows for agentic actions, custom branding per business unit, and multi-language support. Learn how Allianz navigated the full approval process and moved from “not yet” to “go” in six weeks.&lt;/p&gt; 
&lt;p&gt;&lt;strong&gt;&lt;a href="https://registration.awsevents.com/flow/awsevents/reinvent2026/eventcatalog/page/eventcatalog?search=BIZ217" target="_blank" rel="noopener"&gt;BIZ217: One AI assistant, every data source: Unifying access with AWS Quick&lt;/a&gt;&lt;/strong&gt;&lt;br&gt; Wednesday, Dec 2 | 4:00 PM PST&lt;br&gt; &lt;em&gt;Speakers: David Provan, Jess Wang, Jesse Gebhardt&lt;/em&gt;&lt;/p&gt; 
&lt;p&gt;Your data lives in one platform. Your reports live in another. Your documents live somewhere else. Every time someone needs an answer, they spend time figuring out where to look before they can act. Decisions slow down. Teams duplicate work because they cannot find what already exists. New employees take months to become productive because institutional knowledge is trapped in systems they do not know how to navigate. Hear from a customer who solved this by giving their team a single AI assistant that connects across all their enterprise systems. Instead of training people on where to find information, they ask AWS Quick and get answers grounded in their real business data, regardless of where it lives. Learn how they configured the integration, how their teams adopted it, and what changed about the speed and quality of decision-making across the organization.&lt;/p&gt; 
&lt;p&gt;&lt;strong&gt;&lt;a href="https://registration.awsevents.com/flow/awsevents/reinvent2026/eventcatalog/page/eventcatalog?search=BIZ113" target="_blank" rel="noopener"&gt;BIZ113: Getting started with AWS Quick: How a customer went from curious to deployed&lt;/a&gt;&lt;/strong&gt;&lt;br&gt; Thursday, Dec 3 | 12:00 PM PST&lt;br&gt; &lt;em&gt;Speakers: Leo Ohannesian, Justin Bundick, Peter Hunn&lt;/em&gt;&lt;/p&gt; 
&lt;p&gt;Your team spends hours on work that should take minutes: summarizing meetings, drafting documents, finding answers buried across systems, pulling data from multiple platforms. You know AI should help, but you need to see it work in a real environment before you commit. In this session, see how a customer put AWS Quick into daily workflows across their organization and watch live as it composes documents, analyzes data, summarizes conversations, and connects to enterprise systems. Hear what problems they were solving, what changed for their teams after deployment, and what surprised them about adoption. Leave with a clear picture of the problems Quick solves and how to evaluate whether it fits your organization.&lt;/p&gt; 
&lt;p&gt;&lt;strong&gt;&lt;a href="https://registration.awsevents.com/flow/awsevents/reinvent2026/eventcatalog/page/eventcatalog?search=BIZ213" target="_blank" rel="noopener"&gt;BIZ213: Accelerating AI adoption and realized value with AWS Quick&lt;/a&gt;&lt;/strong&gt;&lt;br&gt; Thursday, Dec 3 | 1:30 PM PST&lt;br&gt; &lt;em&gt;Speakers: Neal Cauley, John Brock, Stefan Schulz&lt;/em&gt;&lt;/p&gt; 
&lt;p&gt;You deployed an AI assistant. Your team tried it for a week and stopped. The tool could not access the systems where real work happens, gave generic answers because it was disconnected from your enterprise context, and security would not approve broader rollout because the governance story was unclear. That is where most AI deployments stall. Hear from a customer who lived that exact cycle: low adoption, frustrated leadership, and a growing sense that the investment was wasted. They evaluated multiple alternatives and made the switch to AWS Quick, an AI assistant with governed access to enterprise data, native connectors to the systems their teams already use, and agentic workflows that span business units. Learn what broke in their first deployment, what they looked for in a replacement, and how governed data access turned single-digit adoption into organization-wide usage.&lt;/p&gt; 
&lt;p&gt;&lt;strong&gt;&lt;a href="https://registration.awsevents.com/flow/awsevents/reinvent2026/eventcatalog/page/eventcatalog?search=BIZ215" target="_blank" rel="noopener"&gt;BIZ215: From pilot to enterprise scale with AWS Quick&lt;/a&gt;&lt;/strong&gt;&lt;br&gt; Thursday, Dec 3 | 4:00 PM PST&lt;br&gt; &lt;em&gt;Speakers: Rekha Seshadrinathan, Gayatri Ohri, Michael Murphy&lt;/em&gt;&lt;/p&gt; 
&lt;p&gt;Your AI pilot proved value with a small team. Now you need to deploy across the organization without a dedicated rollout team and without losing what made it work. Hear from a customer who scaled AWS Quick from an initial deployment to thousands of users across their enterprise. Learn the phased strategy they used to expand Quick’s capabilities: connecting teams to enterprise data, automating repetitive workflows, and rolling out agentic features that kept usage growing after the initial adoption wave. Discover what metrics justified continued investment and the operational model they built so scaling Quick did not create a support burden for IT. Walk away with a repeatable framework for taking AWS Quick from proof-of-concept to production deployment across your organization.&lt;/p&gt; 
&lt;p&gt;&lt;strong&gt;&lt;a href="https://registration.awsevents.com/flow/awsevents/reinvent2026/eventcatalog/page/eventcatalog?search=BIZ218" target="_blank" rel="noopener"&gt;BIZ218: From AI tasks to autonomous multi-agent workflows with AWS Quick&lt;/a&gt;&lt;/strong&gt;&lt;br&gt; Thursday, Dec 3 | 4:00 PM PST&lt;br&gt; &lt;em&gt;Speakers: Ramon Lopez, Mike Hogan&lt;/em&gt;&lt;/p&gt; 
&lt;p&gt;The workflows that matter most in financial services span multiple systems, require multiple types of expertise, and today rely on people as the coordination layer. One person pulls data, another reviews it for compliance, a third routes it for approval. That coordination tax is where your team’s time disappears. AWS Quick changes that equation. Instead of one AI assistant doing one task, specialized agents each handle their piece and hand off to the next automatically. The human stays in the loop for judgment calls, not for data gathering and routing. Hear from a customer who moved from standalone AI use cases to compound, multi-agent workflows with AWS Quick. Learn how they identified which workflows to orchestrate first, how they designed agent handoffs that satisfy compliance, and what it means now that the coordination layer runs autonomously.&lt;/p&gt; 
&lt;h2 id="chalk-talks"&gt;Chalk talks&lt;/h2&gt; 
&lt;p&gt;Whiteboard architectures and trade-offs with the AWS Quick product and solutions teams. Bring your toughest questions.&lt;/p&gt; 
&lt;p&gt;&lt;strong&gt;&lt;a href="https://registration.awsevents.com/flow/awsevents/reinvent2026/eventcatalog/page/eventcatalog?search=BIZ306" target="_blank" rel="noopener"&gt;BIZ306: Bring your own ontology: Ground AWS Quick in enterprise context&lt;/a&gt;&lt;/strong&gt;&lt;br&gt; Monday, Nov 30 | 10:00 AM PST&lt;br&gt; &lt;em&gt;Speakers: Tom Ron, Fatima Khakwani&lt;/em&gt;&lt;/p&gt; 
&lt;p&gt;Enterprise questions rarely have answers in a single document. Your business runs on connections: customers to products, teams to processes, and risks to regulations. But vector search doesn’t see any of that. In this chalk talk, we show how to ground AWS Quick in your enterprise context by integrating it with a knowledge graph built from your domain knowledge. We’ll walk through how we built this pattern using GraphRAG for real customers and the results they achieved. Then we’ll open the whiteboard. Bring your toughest use case and we’ll sketch your ontology live, showing how Quick would traverse it to deliver better answers.&lt;/p&gt; 
&lt;p&gt;&lt;strong&gt;&lt;a href="https://registration.awsevents.com/flow/awsevents/reinvent2026/eventcatalog/page/eventcatalog?search=BIZ323" target="_blank" rel="noopener"&gt;BIZ323: Decision frameworks, evaluation, and cost management with AWS Quick&lt;/a&gt;&lt;/strong&gt;&lt;br&gt; Monday, Nov 30 | 10:30 AM PST&lt;br&gt; Tuesday, Dec 1 | 2:30 PM PST &lt;em&gt;(repeat)&lt;/em&gt;&lt;br&gt; &lt;em&gt;Speakers: Sagar Rao, Sandhya Khanderia&lt;/em&gt;&lt;/p&gt; 
&lt;p&gt;Moving from one agent to enterprise-wide automation means choosing the right path (Flows, Automate, Custom Agents, persistent agents) and the right trigger model. In this chalk talk, we whiteboard a decision framework that bakes in evaluation and cost management from the start, not as afterthoughts. We’ll connect orchestration across 25+ connectors, skills, and workstreams to measurable outcomes and dollars. Bring your automation backlog. You’ll leave with a decision matrix, eval harness, and a return on investment (ROI) story.&lt;/p&gt; 
&lt;p&gt;&lt;strong&gt;&lt;a href="https://registration.awsevents.com/flow/awsevents/reinvent2026/eventcatalog/page/eventcatalog?search=BIZ307" target="_blank" rel="noopener"&gt;BIZ307: Architect AI-powered analytics with AWS Quick across your data platforms&lt;/a&gt;&lt;/strong&gt;&lt;br&gt; Monday, Nov 30 | 12:00 PM PST&lt;br&gt; &lt;em&gt;Speakers: Vignessh Baskaran, Jackson Dowden&lt;/em&gt;&lt;/p&gt; 
&lt;p&gt;As organizations adopt AI-powered analytics, they need architectures that connect business intelligence (BI) and conversational AI experiences to their existing data platforms. In this session we’ll diagram architectural patterns for connecting AWS Quick to structured data sources and integrating with third-party catalogs and semantic layers. We’ll compare the available connectivity approaches, including data sources and action connectors based on the Model Context Protocol (MCP), and discuss when to use each. Considerations will span production use cases like BI dashboards, embedded analytics, text-to-SQL Q&amp;amp;A, and agentic workflows. Together we’ll tackle real-world integration challenges including authentication, performance optimization, and governance. This session is ideal for architects and technical leaders looking to build AI-powered analytics across their data platforms.&lt;/p&gt; 
&lt;p&gt;&lt;strong&gt;&lt;a href="https://registration.awsevents.com/flow/awsevents/reinvent2026/eventcatalog/page/eventcatalog?search=BIZ404" target="_blank" rel="noopener"&gt;BIZ404: Teaching Quick to remember and connect with memory and knowledge graphs&lt;/a&gt;&lt;/strong&gt;&lt;br&gt; Monday, Nov 30 | 1:30 PM PST&lt;br&gt; &lt;em&gt;Speakers: Thomas Delteil, Adi Kalyanpur&lt;/em&gt;&lt;/p&gt; 
&lt;p&gt;Every conversation with an AI assistant starts from scratch. Your agent doesn’t remember what worked yesterday, can’t tell you who owns a project without searching again, and repeats the same mistakes because failures are never captured. In this chalk talk, we show how AWS Quick breaks out of statelessness with two primitives: a continuous-learning memory layer where a background agent extracts reusable knowledge after each session and scores it with a probabilistic confidence model, and a personal knowledge graph that connects people, projects, decisions, and evidence across your tools so the agent has a map of your world before it reads a single document. We’ll whiteboard the architecture and the tradeoffs that shaped it, then open it up. Bring your hardest questions about memory staleness, confidence calibration, graph noise, or making agents that actually get better over time.&lt;/p&gt; 
&lt;p&gt;&lt;strong&gt;&lt;a href="https://registration.awsevents.com/flow/awsevents/reinvent2026/eventcatalog/page/eventcatalog?search=BIZ305" target="_blank" rel="noopener"&gt;BIZ305: Security: Zero-trust identity and data protection in the agentic era&lt;/a&gt;&lt;/strong&gt;&lt;br&gt; Monday, Nov 30 | 2:30 PM PST&lt;br&gt; &lt;em&gt;Speakers: Priya Kakarla, Munish Gopal&lt;/em&gt;&lt;/p&gt; 
&lt;p&gt;Security reviews stall AI rollouts more than any capability gap, and agentic AI raises the bar. This chalk talk goes past “is it secure?” to the architecture that gets a CISO to yes. We’ll whiteboard the full identity path: single sign-on (SSO) through providers like Okta and Ping, with end-user identity propagated to the data so authorization is enforced per user, in real time, across 1P AWS and 3P sources. Then we extend that trust to agents: agent identity, deterministic policy checks on every tool call, and data protection: Purview DLP, default guardrails, deny-by-default, and full SIEM auditability.&lt;/p&gt; 
&lt;p&gt;&lt;strong&gt;&lt;a href="https://registration.awsevents.com/flow/awsevents/reinvent2026/eventcatalog/page/eventcatalog?search=BIZ310" target="_blank" rel="noopener"&gt;BIZ310: Secure and scalable multi-tenant deployment patterns for AWS Quick&lt;/a&gt;&lt;/strong&gt;&lt;br&gt; Monday, Nov 30 | 3:00 PM PST&lt;br&gt; &lt;em&gt;Speakers: Chin Rane, Vineet Kachhawaha&lt;/em&gt;&lt;/p&gt; 
&lt;p&gt;As organizations scale their adoption of AWS Quick across business units, subsidiaries, and partner ecosystems, deploying a secure multi-tenant architecture becomes critical. This chalk talk explores proven deployment patterns that balance tenant isolation, governance, and operational efficiency when rolling out AWS Quick at enterprise scale. We will walk through two primary isolation strategies: multi-account separation using AWS Organizations for environment-level boundaries (dev/staging/prod), and namespace-based logical isolation within a single account for user-cohort segmentation.&lt;/p&gt; 
&lt;p&gt;&lt;strong&gt;&lt;a href="https://registration.awsevents.com/flow/awsevents/reinvent2026/eventcatalog/page/eventcatalog?search=BIZ304" target="_blank" rel="noopener"&gt;BIZ304: Building and governing apps on Quick&lt;/a&gt;&lt;/strong&gt;&lt;br&gt; Monday, Nov 30 | 4:00 PM PST&lt;br&gt; &lt;em&gt;Speakers: Shane Thompson, Art Chan&lt;/em&gt;&lt;/p&gt; 
&lt;p&gt;Anyone can generate apps from a sentence. The hard part is governing them and getting them in front of the right users. This chalk talk whiteboards the full lifecycle of AWS Quick Apps: building them from natural language with no separate hosting, wiring in data and actions through MCP, and the three sharing paths: private, public URLs, and enterprise sharing gated behind admin approval workflows.&lt;/p&gt; 
&lt;p&gt;&lt;strong&gt;&lt;a href="https://registration.awsevents.com/flow/awsevents/reinvent2026/eventcatalog/page/eventcatalog?search=BIZ303" target="_blank" rel="noopener"&gt;BIZ303: One context layer, every surface: Enterprise knowledge for agents that work anywhere&lt;/a&gt;&lt;/strong&gt;&lt;br&gt; Thursday, Dec 3 | 12:00 PM PST&lt;br&gt; &lt;em&gt;Speakers: Suren Raju, Josh DeMuth&lt;/em&gt;&lt;/p&gt; 
&lt;p&gt;Your AI agents are only as smart as the data they can reach. In most enterprises, that data is scattered across dozens of systems, siloed, ungoverned, and invisible to the agents that need it most. In this chalk talk, we architect a context layer from the ground up: connecting enterprise knowledge into a unified index, then enriching that foundation with a knowledge graph that maps organizational relationships, business rules, and domain context, giving agents permission-scoped, relationship-aware intelligence at runtime. Then we take that intelligence to where users work: browser, desktop, mobile, collaboration tools, and productivity suites. Each surface independently governed yet sharing one agent identity, one memory, and one set of guardrails. Bring your architecture. We’ll whiteboard the end-to-end path together.&lt;/p&gt; 
&lt;h2 id="workshops"&gt;Workshops&lt;/h2&gt; 
&lt;p&gt;Get hands-on with AWS Quick. Bring your laptop. You’ll leave with something working.&lt;/p&gt; 
&lt;p&gt;&lt;strong&gt;&lt;a href="https://registration.awsevents.com/flow/awsevents/reinvent2026/eventcatalog/page/eventcatalog?search=ctrl%20alt%20ship" target="_blank" rel="noopener"&gt;INV001: Ctrl+Alt+Ship: Hands-On with AWS Quick, Kiro, and Amazon Connect Customer&lt;/a&gt;&lt;/strong&gt;&lt;br&gt; Walk-up, available all week&lt;br&gt; &lt;em&gt;Speakers: Michelle Koh, Anupa Bhattacharyya, Christopher Lott &lt;/em&gt;&lt;/p&gt; 
&lt;p&gt;Ctrl+Alt+Ship is a walk-up, self-paced lab area open all week (Nov 30 – Dec 4) in the Venetian Level 2 ballroom. No reservation is needed and workstations are provided. Attendees pick a track (Kiro, Quick or Connect) and a level, 200 or 400. Both Quick labs are business simulations where Quick connects to the company’s business systems through MCP. In the L200 Kōji Fresh lab, attendees lead the response to a product-safety crisis at a fictional Singapore food company. They investigate across email, CRM, social and supply chain, brief the board and supplier, run a recall across three systems from one instruction, and turn five data exports into board-ready charts. In the L400 SkyNova Airlines lab, 14 passengers have fallen ill overnight and an influencer is blaming the airline. Attendees use Quick across six systems to find the pattern and brief the board. They then rebook stranded passengers within a hard budget, build a Flow that runs the recovery for a grounded flight, and ship a Quick App for the airport transfer desk. Facilitators will be on hand to help.&lt;/p&gt; 
&lt;p&gt;&lt;strong&gt;&lt;a href="https://registration.awsevents.com/flow/awsevents/reinvent2026/eventcatalog/page/eventcatalog?search=BIZ312" target="_blank" rel="noopener"&gt;BIZ312: Build AI-powered analytics with conversational BI on AWS Quick&lt;/a&gt;&lt;/strong&gt;&lt;br&gt; Tuesday, Dec 1 | 3:30 PM PST&lt;br&gt; &lt;em&gt;Speakers: Neeraj Kumar, Salim Khan&lt;/em&gt;&lt;/p&gt; 
&lt;p&gt;Construct a complete AI-powered analytics pipeline, from data ingestion to conversational consumption. Use AWS Quick’s Data Catalog to discover data, query it live without moving it into SPICE, then enrich and import metadata with semantics inherited to build natural-language Q&amp;amp;A. In this hands-on workshop, you start with seven raw banking tables: no catalog, no definitions. An agent inspects the data and builds the catalog for you. You ask questions in plain English and get answers. An agent generates a dashboard showing which top customers are slipping. Another agent, aware of the bank’s compliance rules, checks every offer before it ships. Then you automate the full response as a Quick Flow and wrap it in a custom app your team can use. You leave with a working banking command center that agents built, and that you can rebuild in your own account using conversational analytics.&lt;/p&gt; 
&lt;p&gt;&lt;strong&gt;&lt;a href="https://registration.awsevents.com/flow/awsevents/reinvent2026/eventcatalog/page/eventcatalog?search=BIZ313" target="_blank" rel="noopener"&gt;BIZ313: Build agentic automations for business processes with AWS Quick Automate&lt;/a&gt;&lt;/strong&gt;&lt;br&gt; Wednesday, Dec 2 | 12:00 PM PST&lt;br&gt; &lt;em&gt;Speakers: Reagan Rosario, Zuhayr Raghib&lt;/em&gt;&lt;/p&gt; 
&lt;p&gt;In this session, learn how to build an automation using AWS Quick Automate. Through a financial services example, explore how specialized AI agents work together to handle complex interactions across web pages and business applications. You’ll create a production-ready automation featuring custom agents that use knowledge and tools to transform a merchant onboarding process. Using Quick Automate’s chat-based authoring and visual studio, you’ll configure a workflow with multiple agents, integrate with multiple tools, test and debug the workflow, and then deploy it using robust enterprise controls. Walk away knowing how to develop agentic automations for real-world use cases.&lt;/p&gt; 
&lt;p&gt;&lt;strong&gt;&lt;a href="https://registration.awsevents.com/flow/awsevents/reinvent2026/eventcatalog/page/eventcatalog?search=BIZ311" target="_blank" rel="noopener"&gt;BIZ311: Master agent skills: From desktop builds to production deployment&lt;/a&gt;&lt;/strong&gt;&lt;br&gt; Wednesday, Dec 2 | 3:00 PM PST&lt;br&gt; &lt;em&gt;Speakers: Jed Lechner, Leo Mentis Raj Selvaraj&lt;/em&gt;&lt;/p&gt; 
&lt;p&gt;Build, govern, and scale production-ready agent skills across your enterprise on AWS Quick. Author custom skills on Quick, then shift to the admin perspective: register skills in Quick Catalog for org-wide discovery, configure access policies, and schedule autonomous execution at scale. You’ll deploy measurable automation through two business-driven use cases: churn prevention from contact center data, and intelligent claims processing that eliminates manual back-office work. Leave with working skills, a governance framework for hundreds of agents across teams, and a playbook for communicating ROI to leadership.&lt;/p&gt; 
&lt;p&gt;&lt;strong&gt;&lt;a href="https://registration.awsevents.com/flow/awsevents/reinvent2026/eventcatalog/page/eventcatalog?search=BIZ314" target="_blank" rel="noopener"&gt;BIZ314: Build multi-agent workflows with AWS Quick, MCP, and AgentCore&lt;/a&gt;&lt;/strong&gt;&lt;br&gt; Thursday, Dec 3 | 8:30 AM PST&lt;br&gt; &lt;em&gt;Speakers: Vishnu Elangovan, Josh Anderson&lt;/em&gt;&lt;/p&gt; 
&lt;p&gt;Design and build a multi-agent system inside AWS Quick where specialized agents collaborate to automate complex business processes. Connect to enterprise systems through MCP servers on Amazon Bedrock AgentCore, implement inter-agent communication, configure OAuth 2.0 authentication, and deploy autonomous workflows using Quick Flows. Modeled after production deployments running 12+ coordinated agents across enterprise resource planning (ERP), finance, and procurement, you’ll leave with a working multi-agent solution you can replicate in your own environment.&lt;/p&gt; 
&lt;p&gt;&lt;strong&gt;&lt;a href="https://registration.awsevents.com/flow/awsevents/reinvent2026/eventcatalog/page/eventcatalog?search=BIZ402" target="_blank" rel="noopener"&gt;BIZ402: Configure security and governance controls for AWS Quick&lt;/a&gt;&lt;/strong&gt;&lt;br&gt; Thursday, Dec 3 | 12:00 PM PST&lt;br&gt; &lt;em&gt;Speakers: Leo Mentis Raj Selvaraj, Marshall Bunch&lt;/em&gt;&lt;/p&gt; 
&lt;p&gt;Implement comprehensive security and administrative controls for AWS Quick. Practice configuring identity integration, access policies, and data governance frameworks. Learn to establish monitoring, auditing, and compliance controls that meet enterprise requirements. Work through scenarios covering user management, content governance, and security best practices for AWS Quick environments in production.&lt;/p&gt; 
&lt;h2 id="builders-sessions"&gt;Builders’ sessions&lt;/h2&gt; 
&lt;p&gt;Build alongside AWS experts in small-group, code-level sessions. Bring your laptop and be ready to build.&lt;/p&gt; 
&lt;p&gt;&lt;strong&gt;&lt;a href="https://registration.awsevents.com/flow/awsevents/reinvent2026/eventcatalog/page/eventcatalog?search=BIZ301" target="_blank" rel="noopener"&gt;BIZ301: Build and ship an AI-powered app with AWS Quick&lt;/a&gt;&lt;/strong&gt;&lt;br&gt; Monday, Nov 30 | 2:30 PM PST&lt;br&gt; &lt;em&gt;Speakers: Roy Yung, Oyin Oguntoye, Mei Liu, Vaidy Janardhanam, Douglas Bergquist&lt;/em&gt;&lt;/p&gt; 
&lt;p&gt;In this hands-on builder session, you’ll use AWS Quick to design, iterate on, and publish a fully functional application through natural-language conversation. You’ll connect to a live data source (pre-provisioned through MCP), shape your app interactively with generative AI, persist state across users, and publish something your team or customers can use immediately. Whether you’re accelerating decision-making, automating operational workflows, or putting insights directly in the hands of end users, this session shows how Quick Apps collapses the gap between having an idea and shipping a solution. Bring your laptop and be ready to build.&lt;/p&gt; 
&lt;p&gt;&lt;strong&gt;&lt;a href="https://registration.awsevents.com/flow/awsevents/reinvent2026/eventcatalog/page/eventcatalog?search=BIZ302" target="_blank" rel="noopener"&gt;BIZ302: Embed AWS Quick chat agents in enterprise applications&lt;/a&gt;&lt;/strong&gt;&lt;br&gt; Thursday, Dec 3 | 10:00 AM PST&lt;br&gt; &lt;em&gt;Speakers: Arun Santhosh, Sujit Singh, Anneline Sibanda, Manasi Karale, Ashok Dasineni&lt;/em&gt;&lt;/p&gt; 
&lt;p&gt;Deploy a fully serverless, production-ready solution that embeds AWS Quick agents and dashboards into your enterprise web applications. Using AWS Cloud Development Kit (AWS CDK), configure Amazon Cognito OAuth 2.0 authentication, AWS Lambda, Amazon API Gateway, Amazon CloudFront, and AWS WAF to create a secure embedding architecture. Implement the Quick Embedding SDK, configure domain security and token validation, and deploy a working chat agent accessible from any enterprise application, turning Quick into the conversational AI layer that meets users where they already work.&lt;/p&gt;

			</item>
		<item>
		<title>How LoanPro built an agentic AI loan servicing assistant with Amazon Bedrock AgentCore</title>
		<link>https://aws.amazon.com/blogs/machine-learning/how-loanpro-built-an-agentic-ai-loan-servicing-assistant-with-amazon-bedrock-agentcore/</link>
		
		<dc:creator><![CDATA[Mariah Miller]]></dc:creator>
		<pubDate>Tue, 17 Nov 2026 17:01:08 +0000</pubDate>
				<category><![CDATA[Advanced (300)]]></category>
		<category><![CDATA[Amazon Bedrock]]></category>
		<category><![CDATA[Amazon Bedrock AgentCore]]></category>
		<category><![CDATA[Customer Solutions]]></category>
		<guid isPermaLink="false">869be9515dd4a9d6d29ada060383aebbdf19f1d9</guid>

					<description>LoanPro worked with AWS Partner AllCloud and the AWS Generative AI Innovation Center to build a multi-agent AI assistant on Amazon Bedrock and Amazon Bedrock AgentCore. It gives loan servicing agents instant borrower context and next-best-action recommendations, reducing customer call times by up to 15 percent.</description>
										<content:encoded>&lt;p&gt;&lt;em&gt;This post is co-written with Mariah Miller from AWS and Colin Terry from LoanPro&lt;/em&gt;&lt;/p&gt; 
&lt;p&gt;Have you ever wondered what the experience is like for the person on the other end of the phone when you call your bank for support? When a borrower calls a lender, the answering agent must pull account data from dozens of fields, transaction histories, and workflow queues to orient themselves before they can help the borrower. All the while, the clock runs. Assembling that context by hand means longer calls, inconsistent service quality, and agent frustration. A loan servicing assistant built on agentic AI changes that.&lt;/p&gt; 
&lt;p&gt;&lt;a href="https://www.loanpro.io/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"&gt;LoanPro&lt;/a&gt;, the operating system for modern lenders, set out to change that. Working with AWS Partner &lt;a href="https://allcloud.io/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"&gt;AllCloud&lt;/a&gt; and the &lt;a href="https://aws.amazon.com/ai/generative-ai/innovation-center/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"&gt;AWS Generative AI Innovation Center&lt;/a&gt;, LoanPro built a multi-agent AI assistant that gives servicing agents immediate access to synthesized borrower context and actionable next-step recommendations. Early results show customer call times reduced by up to 15 percent, according to AllCloud’s published case study.&lt;/p&gt; 
&lt;p&gt;In this post, we walk through the business challenge, the agentic AI architecture built on &lt;a href="https://aws.amazon.com/bedrock/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"&gt;Amazon Bedrock&lt;/a&gt; and &lt;a href="https://aws.amazon.com/bedrock/agentcore/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"&gt;Amazon Bedrock AgentCore&lt;/a&gt;, and the outcomes LoanPro achieved. Amazon Bedrock is a fully managed service that offers a choice of high-performing foundation models from leading AI companies through a single API, along with the capabilities you need to build generative AI applications with security, privacy, and responsible AI.&lt;/p&gt; 
&lt;h2 id="about-loanpro"&gt;About LoanPro&lt;/h2&gt; 
&lt;p&gt;LoanPro is a modern, API-first lending and credit platform that automates the entire loan lifecycle from origination to payoff. Founded in 2015, LoanPro serves as the system of record for lenders of every type: fintechs, banks and credit unions, and corporates that offer lending as a value-added service. According to LoanPro, the platform serves over 450 lenders, with loans managed on its platform reaching about 31 percent of US households. The platform powers loan management at enterprise scale across the financial services industry.&lt;/p&gt; 
&lt;p&gt;LoanPro isn’t a lender. It’s the operating system that lenders run on. That distinction matters because LoanPro’s AI strategy is designed to benefit the institutions it serves across the platform, not a single lending operation.&lt;/p&gt; 
&lt;h2 id="the-challenge"&gt;The challenge&lt;/h2&gt; 
&lt;p&gt;One of LoanPro’s core user groups is servicing agents: the people who manage borrower accounts, handle inbound calls, process payments, and resolve issues. During a live customer interaction, an agent might need to review:&lt;/p&gt; 
&lt;ul&gt; 
 &lt;li&gt;The borrower’s complete payment history, including missed and returned payments.&lt;/li&gt; 
 &lt;li&gt;Active loan terms, fees, and current balance.&lt;/li&gt; 
 &lt;li&gt;Prior interactions and open support tickets.&lt;/li&gt; 
 &lt;li&gt;Available workflow actions such as payment plans, deferrals, or escalations.&lt;/li&gt; 
&lt;/ul&gt; 
&lt;p&gt;Before the AI assistant, agents navigated this information manually, moving through multiple screens and tabs to assemble the full picture. Less experienced agents took longer, and outcomes varied depending on who picked up the phone. LoanPro needed a solution that could:&lt;/p&gt; 
&lt;ul&gt; 
 &lt;li&gt;Instantly synthesize account data into a coherent summary.&lt;/li&gt; 
 &lt;li&gt;Recommend the right next action based on account context.&lt;/li&gt; 
 &lt;li&gt;Deliver more consistent servicing quality across agents of different experience levels.&lt;/li&gt; 
&lt;/ul&gt; 
&lt;h2 id="solution-overview"&gt;Solution overview&lt;/h2&gt; 
&lt;p&gt;With the AWS Generative AI Innovation Center, LoanPro partnered with AllCloud to design and build a multi-agent AI assistant using &lt;a href="https://aws.amazon.com/bedrock/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"&gt;Amazon Bedrock&lt;/a&gt;. The assistant runs on Anthropic’s &lt;a href="https://docs.aws.amazon.com/bedrock/latest/userguide/model-card-anthropic-claude-sonnet-5-5.html" target="_blank" rel="noopener"&gt;Claude Sonnet 5.5&lt;/a&gt;, &lt;a href="https://docs.aws.amazon.com/bedrock/latest/userguide/model-card-anthropic-claude-haiku-5-5.html" target="_blank" rel="noopener"&gt;Claude Haiku 5.5&lt;/a&gt;, and &lt;a href="https://docs.aws.amazon.com/bedrock/latest/userguide/model-card-anthropic-claude-opus-5-5.html" target="_blank" rel="noopener"&gt;Claude Opus 5.5&lt;/a&gt; on Amazon Bedrock (see &lt;a href="https://docs.aws.amazon.com/bedrock/latest/userguide/model-cards.html" target="_blank" rel="noopener"&gt;Models at a glance in Amazon Bedrock&lt;/a&gt;). Model availability varies by AWS Region. For model availability by Region, refer to &lt;a href="https://docs.aws.amazon.com/bedrock/latest/userguide/models-region-compatibility.html" target="_blank" rel="noopener"&gt;Regional availability by models in Amazon Bedrock&lt;/a&gt;. It’s orchestrated with &lt;a href="https://aws.amazon.com/bedrock/agentcore/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"&gt;Amazon Bedrock AgentCore&lt;/a&gt;, a platform to build, connect, and optimize agents at scale, with any framework or model. Agent logic is built with the &lt;a href="https://strandsagents.com/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"&gt;Strands Agents SDK&lt;/a&gt;, an open source framework for building AI agents with tool use. The system coordinates specialized agents through a composer that routes each request to the right specialist, so each agent handles a focused task and can be evaluated and improved independently. The team chose these managed services over building the agent runtime and model access in-house. Amazon Bedrock provides access to leading foundation models through a single API, giving the team flexibility to adopt new models without managing separate endpoints. Amazon Bedrock AgentCore provides a managed agent runtime that removes the operational overhead of running agents in production.&lt;/p&gt; 
&lt;p&gt;Figure 1 shows this flow in implementation terms. A user request enters through LoanPro API v2 on &lt;a href="https://docs.aws.amazon.com/apigateway/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"&gt;Amazon API Gateway&lt;/a&gt;. An &lt;a href="https://docs.aws.amazon.com/lambda/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"&gt;AWS Lambda&lt;/a&gt; handler places the request on an &lt;a href="https://docs.aws.amazon.com/sqs/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"&gt;Amazon Simple Queue Service (Amazon SQS)&lt;/a&gt; queue. The Agent Interceptor Lambda function then invokes the Loan Summary Agent. The agent runs on Amazon Bedrock AgentCore runtime, a capability of Amazon Bedrock AgentCore, and uses Anthropic’s Claude models on Amazon Bedrock. It reaches its tools through a &lt;a href="https://modelcontextprotocol.io/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"&gt;Model Context Protocol (MCP)&lt;/a&gt; server on Amazon Bedrock AgentCore Gateway, a capability of Amazon Bedrock AgentCore. The gateway finds the right tool in a catalog that the Loan Summary Service registers in AWS Systems Manager &lt;a href="https://docs.aws.amazon.com/systems-manager/latest/userguide/systems-manager-parameter-store.html" target="_blank" rel="noopener"&gt;Parameter Store&lt;/a&gt;. The Loan Summary Tool Lambda function validates the caller’s signature against LoanPro’s single sign-on (SSO) service. It then retrieves account data from LoanPro’s loan management system, which runs on &lt;a href="https://docs.aws.amazon.com/ec2/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"&gt;Amazon Elastic Compute Cloud (Amazon EC2)&lt;/a&gt; behind a &lt;a href="https://docs.aws.amazon.com/elasticloadbalancing/latest/network/introduction.html" target="_blank" rel="noopener"&gt;Network Load Balancer&lt;/a&gt;. The system’s role-based access controls validate the same signature. LoanPro also checks each request against the user’s permissions and compliance controls, then writes the action to its audit trail. The sections that follow describe these pieces: the multi-agent system, the tools it calls, and the gateway.&lt;/p&gt; 
&lt;div style="width: 810px" class="wp-caption alignnone"&gt;
 &lt;a href="https://d2908q01vomqb2.cloudfront.net/f1f836cb4ea6efb2a0b1b99f41ad8b103eff4b59/2026/11/11/ML-21891-1.png" target="_blank" rel="noopener"&gt;&lt;img src="https://d2908q01vomqb2.cloudfront.net/f1f836cb4ea6efb2a0b1b99f41ad8b103eff4b59/2026/11/11/ML-21891-1.png" alt="LoanPro AI servicing architecture on Amazon Bedrock and Amazon Bedrock AgentCore (LoanPro engineering, October 2026): API Gateway → Handler Lambda → SQS → Agent Interceptor → AgentCore runtime → Gateway → Parameter Store catalog → Loan Summary Tool → SSO/JWK → NLB/EC2 with RBAC" width="800"&gt;&lt;/a&gt;
 &lt;p class="wp-caption-text"&gt;Figure 1: High-level architecture of the LoanPro AI servicing assistant&lt;/p&gt;
&lt;/div&gt; 
&lt;p&gt;The architecture has four key components, which combine the pieces in Figure 1 with the composer agent and Invoke API Tool that coordinate and act on them:&lt;/p&gt; 
&lt;h3 id="multi-agent-architecture-with-strands-agents-sdk"&gt;Multi-agent architecture with Strands Agents SDK&lt;/h3&gt; 
&lt;p&gt;The solution uses a multi-agent architecture built with the Strands Agents SDK. Rather than a single monolithic model handling every task, specialized agents collaborate to serve different parts of the servicing workflow. One agent focuses on data retrieval and account summarization. Another handles action selection and workflow recommendations. A composer agent routes each request to the right specialist and composes the final response. This separation of concerns makes each agent more straightforward to evaluate, tune, and extend independently.&lt;/p&gt; 
&lt;h3 id="tool-use-data-retrieval-and-invoke-api"&gt;Tool use: Data Retrieval and Invoke API&lt;/h3&gt; 
&lt;p&gt;The agents retrieve borrower data from LoanPro’s internal systems through &lt;strong&gt;tool use&lt;/strong&gt;, a capability in Amazon Bedrock for calling external functions and APIs. Two primary tools power the assistant:&lt;/p&gt; 
&lt;ul&gt; 
 &lt;li&gt;&lt;strong&gt;Data Retrieval Tool:&lt;/strong&gt; Fetches account details, payment histories, loan terms, and borrower metadata from LoanPro’s data layer, shown in Figure 1 as the Loan Summary Tool. It spans eight distinct API endpoints, synthesizing around 1,400 fields per account.&lt;/li&gt; 
 &lt;li&gt;&lt;strong&gt;Invoke API Tool:&lt;/strong&gt; Triggers workflow actions within LoanPro’s platform, such as presenting informational panels to the agent or initiating a predefined automation sequence.&lt;/li&gt; 
&lt;/ul&gt; 
&lt;p&gt;By connecting to LoanPro’s existing APIs, the assistant works with live production data without requiring a separate data pipeline or data warehouse.&lt;/p&gt; 
&lt;h3 id="amazon-bedrock-agentcore-gateway-with-aws-lambda"&gt;Amazon Bedrock AgentCore Gateway with AWS Lambda&lt;/h3&gt; 
&lt;p&gt;AgentCore Gateway is the secure access layer between the agents and the tools they call. It exposes LoanPro’s APIs to the agents as tools over the Model Context Protocol (MCP), handles authentication, and manages those connections. &lt;a href="https://aws.amazon.com/lambda/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"&gt;AWS Lambda&lt;/a&gt;, a serverless compute service that runs code without provisioning or managing servers, provides the integration layer between the gateway and LoanPro’s APIs. The team chose AgentCore Gateway with Lambda over a direct integration for three reasons. It centralizes authentication at a managed entry point, isolates LoanPro’s APIs behind a managed boundary, and lets new tools be added without redeploying the agents. This serverless approach keeps the infrastructure lightweight and scales automatically with request volume. In Figure 1, the Agent Interceptor (an AWS Lambda function) invokes the agent on AgentCore runtime. AgentCore Gateway enforces this access control, screening each request before it reaches the Loan Summary Tool. Because the assistant operates in a regulated lending context, the production deployment pairs these components with additional controls. LoanPro’s role-based access controls and compliance guardrails apply to each request, and AI actions are written to LoanPro’s audit trail and attributed to a named, responsible person.&lt;/p&gt; 
&lt;h3 id="integration-into-loanpros-existing-web-portal"&gt;Integration into LoanPro’s existing web portal&lt;/h3&gt; 
&lt;p&gt;The assistant is embedded directly into LoanPro’s existing web portal, the same interface servicing agents already use. Agents interact with the AI assistant through two components:&lt;/p&gt; 
&lt;ul&gt; 
 &lt;li&gt;&lt;strong&gt;Account summary view:&lt;/strong&gt; When an agent opens a borrower account, the assistant automatically generates a narrative summary of the account’s health, recent activity, and outstanding issues. Raw data becomes a coherent story about the borrower’s situation.&lt;/li&gt; 
 &lt;li&gt;&lt;strong&gt;Conversational chat interface:&lt;/strong&gt; Agents can ask follow-up questions in natural language to dig deeper into specific account details. The team describes this as allowing agents to “talk to the loan.” Every agent interaction with the chat interface is logged, maintaining an auditable record of actions taken during a borrower conversation.&lt;/li&gt; 
&lt;/ul&gt; 
&lt;h2 id="implementation-approach"&gt;Implementation approach&lt;/h2&gt; 
&lt;p&gt;LoanPro built the assistant with the &lt;a href="https://aws.amazon.com/ai/generative-ai/innovation-center/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"&gt;AWS Generative AI Innovation Center&lt;/a&gt;, which pairs AWS applied scientists with customers and partners to accelerate generative AI initiatives, and AllCloud, an AWS Premier Consulting Partner. The three teams designed and built the solution together. What began as a focused engagement now runs in production, serving LoanPro’s servicing agents.&lt;/p&gt; 
&lt;p&gt;The work was organized into two parallel workstreams:&lt;/p&gt; 
&lt;h3 id="workstream-1-servicing-agent-assistant"&gt;Workstream 1: Servicing agent assistant&lt;/h3&gt; 
&lt;p&gt;The primary workstream built the AI assistant that is now running in production. Key activities included:&lt;/p&gt; 
&lt;ul&gt; 
 &lt;li&gt;Selecting and mapping the data sources (application data, loan histories, borrower records) the agent would need to retrieve.&lt;/li&gt; 
 &lt;li&gt;Developing agent functionality for autonomous data retrieval and intelligent action selection.&lt;/li&gt; 
 &lt;li&gt;Iterating on prompts to improve retrieval accuracy and output quality.&lt;/li&gt; 
 &lt;li&gt;Documenting LoanPro’s security model and integration patterns to inform the production roadmap.&lt;/li&gt; 
&lt;/ul&gt; 
&lt;h3 id="workstream-2-llm-account-rule-automation-advisory"&gt;Workstream 2: LLM account rule automation advisory&lt;/h3&gt; 
&lt;p&gt;LoanPro had an existing proof of concept (POC) that used a large language model (LLM) to convert natural language into rule automation code for their Clojure rules engine. The core functionality worked, but the solution had known limitations: frequent timeouts, no monitoring, and no deployment strategy. AllCloud conducted architectural reviews and provided recommendations to bring this existing POC to a production-ready state, including a documented production architecture and risk assessment.&lt;/p&gt; 
&lt;h2 id="evaluation-pipeline"&gt;Evaluation pipeline&lt;/h2&gt; 
&lt;p&gt;A structured evaluation pipeline validated agent accuracy against benchmarks established by LoanPro’s subject-matter experts (SMEs). The team assembled a benchmark of 150 SME-labeled accounts. Against that benchmark, they measured the agent’s ability to retrieve data, summarize findings, and select appropriate actions, holding it to a 99 percent accuracy target that the agent met. This framework carries forward into production, giving LoanPro a repeatable process for measuring and improving agent performance over time.&lt;/p&gt; 
&lt;h2 id="results"&gt;Results&lt;/h2&gt; 
&lt;p&gt;Deploying the AI assistant delivered measurable operational impact:&lt;/p&gt; 
&lt;ul&gt; 
 &lt;li&gt;&lt;strong&gt;Up to 15 percent reduction in customer call times&lt;/strong&gt; (source: AllCloud case study). Across over 800 servicing professionals at LoanPro’s early launch lender partners, agents previously spent 18–20 percent of every call (1–2.5 minutes across 5–7 clicks) orienting to accounts. The AI assistant consolidates that context into a single click, saving 1 to nearly 2 minutes per call. Across those agents, that reclaims up to 9 minutes per operating hour per agent, roughly 120 hours of agent capacity every operating hour.&lt;/li&gt; 
 &lt;li&gt;&lt;strong&gt;Decreased internal ticket volumes by nearly 8 percent&lt;/strong&gt; (source: LoanPro). Lenders on the platform report fewer support escalations as agents resolve more cases during the initial call.&lt;/li&gt; 
 &lt;li&gt;&lt;strong&gt;Account summaries in under 15 seconds&lt;/strong&gt; (source: LoanPro internal benchmarks). The narrative summary transforms raw borrower data into a coherent account story within 15 seconds of opening an account, replacing what previously took agents 3–5 minutes of manual navigation.&lt;/li&gt; 
 &lt;li&gt;&lt;strong&gt;Consistent servicing quality&lt;/strong&gt;. The assistant provides the same level of account context and action recommendations to agents across the platform, reducing variability tied to individual experience levels.&lt;/li&gt; 
 &lt;li&gt;&lt;strong&gt;Compliance-aligned interaction logging&lt;/strong&gt;. Every action and query the agent makes through the AI assistant is logged, providing an auditable record of interactions during borrower conversations.&lt;/li&gt; 
 &lt;li&gt;&lt;strong&gt;Responsible AI controls&lt;/strong&gt;. &lt;a href="https://aws.amazon.com/bedrock/guardrails/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"&gt;Amazon Bedrock Guardrails&lt;/a&gt; apply content filtering and grounding validation to agent responses alongside LoanPro’s compliance guardrails and role-based access controls (RBAC), keeping outputs within policy before they reach a servicing agent and the borrower.&lt;/li&gt; 
&lt;/ul&gt; 
&lt;blockquote&gt;
 &lt;p&gt;&lt;em&gt;“The AI assistant has changed how servicing agents work. Instead of spending the first few minutes of every call navigating screens and assembling context, agents now open an account and immediately see the full picture with prescriptive recommendations around the next actions to take. That time savings compounds across thousands of calls.”&lt;/em&gt;&lt;/p&gt; 
 &lt;p&gt;— Colin Terry, Chief Product Officer, LoanPro&lt;/p&gt;
&lt;/blockquote&gt; 
&lt;h2 id="looking-ahead"&gt;Looking ahead&lt;/h2&gt; 
&lt;p&gt;Building on the success of the servicing agent assistant, LoanPro and AllCloud are expanding their collaboration. The teams are developing additional AI agents to extend intelligent capabilities across more parts of the lending workflow, moving beyond servicing into areas such as collections and origination support.&lt;/p&gt; 
&lt;p&gt;The modular, multi-agent architecture makes this expansion practical. New agents can be built and deployed independently using the same Amazon Bedrock AgentCore infrastructure, without redesigning the core system. As the Strands Agents SDK and Amazon Bedrock AgentCore continue to evolve, LoanPro plans to incorporate new capabilities as they become available.&lt;/p&gt; 
&lt;h2 id="conclusion"&gt;Conclusion&lt;/h2&gt; 
&lt;p&gt;In this post, we showed how LoanPro, the AWS Generative AI Innovation Center, and AllCloud built a multi-agent AI assistant using Amazon Bedrock and Amazon Bedrock AgentCore to help servicing agents resolve borrower calls faster and more consistently. The solution retrieves data autonomously, generates account summaries in real time, and recommends next-best actions, reducing customer call times by up to 15 percent.&lt;/p&gt; 
&lt;p&gt;If you are building agentic AI solutions for customer-facing workflows, here are some resources to explore:&lt;/p&gt; 
&lt;ul&gt; 
 &lt;li&gt;&lt;a href="https://aws.amazon.com/bedrock/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"&gt;Amazon Bedrock&lt;/a&gt;: Fully managed service for building with foundation models.&lt;/li&gt; 
 &lt;li&gt;&lt;a href="https://aws.amazon.com/bedrock/agentcore/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"&gt;Amazon Bedrock AgentCore&lt;/a&gt;: Infrastructure to deploy, manage, and scale AI agents.&lt;/li&gt; 
 &lt;li&gt;&lt;a href="https://strandsagents.com/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"&gt;Strands Agents SDK&lt;/a&gt;: Open source framework for building AI agents with tool use.&lt;/li&gt; 
 &lt;li&gt;&lt;a href="https://aws.amazon.com/ai/generative-ai/innovation-center/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"&gt;AWS Generative AI Innovation Center&lt;/a&gt;: Accelerate your generative AI journey with AWS experts.&lt;/li&gt; 
&lt;/ul&gt; 
&lt;p&gt;To get started, explore the &lt;a href="https://github.com/strands-agents/sdk-python" target="_blank" rel="noopener"&gt;Strands Agents SDK on GitHub&lt;/a&gt; for building multi-agent systems, review the &lt;a href="https://docs.aws.amazon.com/bedrock/latest/userguide/agentcore.html" target="_blank" rel="noopener"&gt;Amazon Bedrock AgentCore documentation&lt;/a&gt; for deploying agents at scale, or visit the &lt;a href="https://aws.amazon.com/ai/generative-ai/innovation-center/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"&gt;AWS Generative AI Innovation Center&lt;/a&gt; to learn how AWS experts can help accelerate your own AI initiative.&lt;/p&gt; 
&lt;p&gt;&amp;nbsp;&lt;/p&gt;

  &lt;/div&gt; 
  &lt;h3 class="lb-h4"&gt;Mariah Miller&lt;/h3&gt; 
  &lt;p style="overflow: hidden"&gt;Mariah is a Senior Generative AI Strategist at the AWS Generative AI Innovation Center, where she leads AI innovation strategy and the center’s partner delivery program. She takes enterprise generative AI programs from vision to production across media, sports, gaming, and financial services. She also leads the center’s accelerators for multimodal video search and agentic commerce. On this project, she led the solution strategy and customer engagement. Outside of work, Mariah likes to spend time outdoors.&lt;/p&gt; 
 &lt;/div&gt; 
 &lt;div class="blog-author-box" style="padding-top: 2.0em"&gt; 
  &lt;div class="blog-author-image" style="margin-right: 1.0em"&gt;
   &lt;img loading="lazy" class="alignnone size-full" src="https://d2908q01vomqb2.cloudfront.net/f1f836cb4ea6efb2a0b1b99f41ad8b103eff4b59/2026/11/05/ML-21891-3.jpg" alt="Colin Terry" width="100" height="133"&gt;
  &lt;/div&gt; 
  &lt;h3 class="lb-h4"&gt;Colin Terry&lt;/h3&gt; 
  &lt;p style="overflow: hidden"&gt;Colin is Chief Product Officer at LoanPro, where he led the launch of LoanPro Core MCP, the company’s AI gateway for lenders. With a passion for modernizing how lenders build, service, and scale credit, Colin focuses on making AI practical and safe in regulated lending, with layered compliance guardrails that keep AI agents within regulatory boundaries.
